Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The fermi-dirac variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 291 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 656 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 291 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the fermi-dirac structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 656. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
